Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 4601	### Embedding Model
> 4602	`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, `dimensions: 768` (truncated output via OpenRouter).
> 4603	Schema: `vector(768)` — HNSW cosine index.
> 4604	
> 4605	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 4606	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 4607	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 4608	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 4609	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 4610	
> 4611	### Comandos de manutenção da Mesh
> 4612	```bash
> 4613	# Verificar nós sem embedding (deveria retornar array vazio):
> 4614	curl -s "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?embedding=is.null&select=id,name" \
> 4615	  -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(f'{len(d)} nós sem embedding')"
> 4616	
> 4617	# Backfill manual (Bun-native — requer .env):
> 4618	bun run scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts
> 4619	
> 4620	# Backfill de código-fonte nos nós AST:
> 4621	bun run scripts/backfill-node-content.ts
> 4622	
> 4623	# Re-sync AST completo:
> 4624	cd packages/gitnexus-core && npm run sync-mesh
> 4625	
> 4626	# Acompanhar re-ingesta pós-commit:
> 4627	tail -f /tmp/mcorch-ingest.log
> 4628	```

> 4632	## Video Rescue Protocol
> 4633	
> 4634	When a video generation operation (Google Veo) times out or client disconnects:
> 4635	```bash
> 4636	# Via edge function (production):
> 4637	curl -X POST "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/rescue-video" \
> 4638	  -H "Authorization: Bearer <user_jwt>" \
> 4639	  -d '{"content_id":"<uuid>","operation_id":"<operation_name>"}'
> 4640	
> 4641	# Via local script (admin/debug):
> 4642	source .env && bun run rescue.ts <operation_id> <content_id>
> 4643	```

> 4735	## Constellation Matrix Intelligence & Stabilization Record (2026-05-03)
> 4736	
> 4737	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. O motor Sigma.js/ForceAtlas2 apresentava loops de renderização infinitos ("blinking") e resets de estado ao interagir com a sidebar. A correção envolveu a estabilização de referências de props, memoização rigorosa das opções do grafo e desacoplamento de filtros dos ciclos de renderização do canvas. Paralelamente, a Matrix foi dotada de inteligência de código nativa via hook `useNodeIntelligence` e um `NodeDetailsPanel` reestruturado com navegação por abas.

> 1	-- Migration: 20260508025933_delete_account_rpc
> 2	-- Description: LGPD Article 17 self-service account deletion RPC (DD-001 / BR-018)
> 3	-- Security Checklist (FMEA-011):
> 4	--   [x] RLS not required (SECURITY DEFINER function — operates as service role)
> 5	--   [x] Default deny via REVOKE/GRANT pattern below
> 6	--   [x] No PII columns exposed — function deletes PII
> 7	--   [x] No secrets hardcoded
> 8	--   [x] /security-review approved
> 9	
> 10	-- delete_account: cascade-deletes all user-owned data, returns affected table summary
> 11	-- SECURITY DEFINER ensures the function runs with owner privileges, bypassing RLS
> 12	-- Client MUST pass their own user_id; edge function verifies auth.uid() == p_user_id
> 13	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.delete_account(p_user_id uuid)
> 14	RETURNS jsonb
> 15	LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 16	SECURITY DEFINER
> 17	SET search_path = public
> 18	AS $$
> 19	DECLARE
> 20	  v_result jsonb;
> 21	  v_content_count int;
> 22	  v_runs_count int;
> 23	  v_nodes_count int;
> 24	  v_conversations_count int;
> 25	  v_social_count int;
> 26	  v_scheduled_count int;
> 27	BEGIN
> 28	  -- Verify caller is deleting their own account (belt-and-suspenders; edge fn also checks)
> 29	  IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND auth.uid() != p_user_id THEN
> 30	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Unauthorized: cannot delete another user''s account';
> 31	  END IF;
> 32	
> 33	  -- Count rows before deletion for audit summary
> 34	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_content_count      FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 35	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_runs_count         FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 36	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_nodes_count        FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 37	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_conversations_count FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 38	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_social_count       FROM social_accounts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 39	  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_scheduled_count    FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 40	
> 41	  -- Cascade delete in dependency order (child tables first)
> 42	  DELETE FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 43	  DELETE FROM social_accounts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 44	  DELETE FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 45	  DELETE FROM mcorch_edges       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 46	  DELETE FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 47	  DELETE FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 48	  DELETE FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 49	  DELETE FROM user_api_keys      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;
> 50	  DELETE FROM profiles           WHERE id = p_user_id;
> 51	
> 52	  v_result := jsonb_build_object(
> 53	    'deleted', true,
> 54	    'user_id', p_user_id,
> 55	    'tables_affected', jsonb_build_object(
> 56	      'content_library',    v_content_count,
> 57	      'pipeline_runs',      v_runs_count,
> 58	      'mcorch_nodes',       v_nodes_count,
> 59	      'aios_conversations', v_conversations_count,
> 60	      'social_accounts',    v_social_count,
> 61	      'scheduled_posts',    v_scheduled_count
> 62	    )
> 63	  );
> 64	
> 65	  RETURN v_result;
> 66	END;
> 67	$$;
> 68	
> 69	-- Only authenticated users can call this function (edge fn further restricts to own user_id)
> 70	REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.delete_account(uuid) FROM PUBLIC;
> 71	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.delete_account(uuid) TO authenticated;
> 72	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.delete_account(uuid) TO service_role;
> 73	
> ```

set`).\n\n**Alternative:** Sovereign passes keys directly in conversation OR retrieves from `.env` (Higgsfield is in `.env` already).\n\n**Success signal:** REST GET `user_api_keys?user_id=eq.<sovereign>` returns all 6 keys SET.\n\n### Step 6 \u2014 Verification gates (must ALL pass before declaring success)\n\n| Gate | Check | Pass criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| G1 | Sovereign can still log in | `/auth` \u2192 success \u2192 `/dashboard` |\n| G2 | Profile row intact | `profiles?id=eq.<sovereign>` returns row with `mco_balance=10000, score=0` |\n| G3 | All target tables empty | Each Sovereign-filtered query returns 0 rows |\n| G4 | OAuth integrations intact | `social_accounts?user_id=eq.<sovereign>&is_active=eq.true` returns 3+ rows |\n| G5 | Per-user vault preserved + expanded | `user_api_keys?user_id=eq.<sovereign>` returns row with all keys SET |\n| G6 | Canvas Studio portfolio intact | `vm_canvas_executions?user_id=eq.<sovereign>` returns 85 rows |\n| G7 | Audit trail intact | `mcorch_nodes?user_id=is.null&node_type=in.(handoff,milestone,decision)` count unchanged |\n| G8 | Ledger empty | `mcoin_transactions?user_id=eq.<sovereign>` returns [] |\n| G9 | TS still clean | `npx tsc --noEmit` zero errors |\n| G10 | Tests still pass | `bun run test` 189/189 |\n\n---\n\n## Recovery path (rollback)\n\n**If Step 4 RPC fails mid-transaction:**\n- RPC is wrapped in implicit transaction (`LANGUAGE plpgsql` body runs atomic). Postgres rolls back automatically. No action needed.\n\n**If Step 4 succeeds but a downstream gate (G1\u2013G10) fails:**\n1. Stop. Do NOT proceed to Phase 2 (E2E).\n2. Restore from backups in `.claude/context/backups/great-reset-2026-05-27/`:\n   ```bash\n   for f in .claude/context/backups/great-reset-2026-05-27/*.json; do\n     table=$(basename \"$f\" .json)\n     # Bulk INSERT via REST or Supabase CLI seed\n   done\n   ```\n3. Reset `mco_balance` to backed-up value via PATCH `profiles?id=eq.<sovereign>`.\n4. Open incident report in `infra_health_logs` with `service=great-reset-rollback`.\n\n**If verification gate G1 (login) fails:**\n- Sovereign manually re-authenticates via OAuth (Google). Profile row preserved means auth works; only `last_seen_at` may reset.\n\n---\n\n## Success signal (whole protocol)\n\nAll 10 verification gates pass + Sovereign confirms via `/dashboard` UI that:\n1. Login works\n2. Balance shows 10000 mcoCoins\n3. LinkedIn/Instagram/Facebook integration icons still green\n4. Canvas Studio shows existing portfolio\n5. `/dashboard/orchestration` canvas is empty (no prior pipeline runs)\n6. `/dashboard/constellation` shows clean state (no Sovereign-owned conversation nodes)\n\nThen proceed to Phase 2 (Health Sweep).\n\n---\n\n## Anti-patterns prohibited\n\n- \u274c Skipping Step 1 (backup) \"because it's a small dataset\" \u2014 Lei 1 requires material recovery path.\n- \u274c Running DELETEs ad-hoc via separate REST calls instead of atomic RPC \u2014 increases blast radius if interrupted mid-flight.\n- \u274c Skipping `npx tsc --noEmit` + `bun run test` post-reset (G9 + G10) \u2014 schema drift might break code silently.\n- \u274c Hardcoding `mco_balance` value in migration \u2014 use parameter `p_new_balance` so future resets are configurable.\n- \u274c Granting EXECUTE to `authenticated` role \u2014 this is admin-only operation; LGPD `delete_account` is the user-facing self-service.\n\n---\n\n## Audit observation (post-execution requirement)\n\nInsert observation node in `mcorch_nodes` with `user_id IS NULL` (system-owned) and metadata:\n```json\n{\n  \"kind\": \"great-reset\",\n  \"target_user_id\": \"ada39fae-...\",\n  \"executed_at\": \"<ISO timestamp>\",\n  \"tables_affected\": { \"...\": \"...\" },\n  \"operator\": \"MCORCH Master Execution Agent\",\n  \"reviewer\": \"Sovereign\",\n  \"owner\": \"Sovereign\",\n  \"session_seal\": \"v6.10.0\"\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Connection to Survival Laws\n\n- **Lei 1 (Materialidade):** Every step has explicit material proof (JSON dumps, REST counts, JSONB return). No \"reset succeeded\" claim is allowed without paired evidence.\n- **Lei 2 (Anticipated Process):** This SOP is written BEFORE code execution (gate enforced).\n- **Lei 3 (Pruning):** Backup files are kept in `.claude/context/backups/`, NOT loaded into active context after dump.\n- **Lei 4 (ORO):** Triplet declared in header.\n"
}

  "content": "-- Migration: 20260527004739_soft_reset_account_rpc\n-- Description: Soft Reset RPC \u2014 wipes user-generated content/history/ledger while preserving profile, credentials, and Canvas Studio portfolio.\n-- Use case: Admin-driven \"Great Reset\" to enable clean E2E validation on an existing account (Usu\u00e1rio Zero pattern).\n-- Sibling of public.delete_account (LGPD). Key differences:\n--   * PRESERVES: profiles, user_api_keys, social_accounts, vm_canvas_executions, vm_canvas_assets, affiliate_config\n--   * ADDS: mcoin_transactions, content_mesh_asset, affiliate_links (defensive)\n--   * UPDATES: profiles.mco_balance to parameterized new balance + score=0\n-- Security Checklist:\n--   [x] RLS not required (SECURITY DEFINER \u2014 operates as service role)\n--   [x] EXECUTE granted to service_role ONLY (NOT authenticated) \u2014 admin operation\n--   [x] No PII exposure (function deletes/preserves; never returns sensitive payload)\n--   [x] No secrets hardcoded\n--   [x] Atomic transaction guaranteed by plpgsql body\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(\n  p_user_id uuid,\n  p_new_balance int DEFAULT 10000\n)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  v_result jsonb;\n  v_content_count int;\n  v_runs_count int;\n  v_nodes_count int;\n  v_edges_count int;\n  v_conversations_count int;\n  v_scheduled_count int;\n  v_transactions_count int;\n  v_content_mesh_count int;\n  v_affiliate_links_count int;\n  v_profile_exists boolean;\nBEGIN\n  -- Pre-flight: ensure profile exists (soft reset on non-existent user is invalid)\n  SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE id = p_user_id) INTO v_profile_exists;\n  IF NOT v_profile_exists THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'soft_reset_account: profile % not found', p_user_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- Count rows before deletion for audit summary\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_content_count          FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_runs_count             FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_nodes_count            FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_edges_count            FROM mcorch_edges       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_conversations_count    FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_scheduled_count        FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_transactions_count     FROM mcoin_transactions WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_content_mesh_count     FROM content_mesh_asset WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_affiliate_links_count  FROM affiliate_links    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n\n  -- Cascade delete in dependency order (child tables first)\n  DELETE FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM affiliate_links    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM content_mesh_asset WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcorch_edges       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcoin_transactions WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n\n  -- Restore profile state (preserve row, reset balance + score)\n  UPDATE profiles\n     SET mco_balance = p_new_balance,\n         score       = 0,\n         updated_at  = now()\n   WHERE id = p_user_id;\n\n  -- PRESERVED (NOT deleted):\n  --   * profiles (row preserved, balance + score reset above)\n  --   * user_api_keys (per-user vault retained)\n  --   * social_accounts (OAuth tokens retained for LinkedIn/Instagram/Facebook/Twitter)\n  --   * affiliate_config (ML affiliate channel retained)\n  --   * vm_canvas_executions, vm_canvas_assets (Canvas Studio portfolio retained)\n  --   * crew_module_ownership (if any \u2014 system-level assignments)\n  --   * infra_health_logs (global telemetry)\n  --   * mcorch_nodes/mcorch_edges with user_id IS NULL (system audit trail preserved)\n\n  v_result := jsonb_build_object(\n    'reset', true,\n    'user_id', p_user_id,\n    'new_balance', p_new_balance,\n    'tables_affected', jsonb_build_object(\n      'content_library',    v_content_count,\n      'pipeline_runs',      v_runs_count,\n      'mcorch_nodes',       v_nodes_count,\n      'mcorch_edges',       v_edges_count,\n      'aios_conversations', v_conversations_count,\n      'scheduled_posts',    v_scheduled_count,\n      'mcoin_transactions', v_transactions_count,\n      'content_mesh_asset', v_content_mesh_count,\n      'affiliate_links',    v_affiliate_links_count\n    ),\n    'preserved_tables', jsonb_build_array(\n      'profiles', 'user_api_keys', 'social_accounts',\n      'affiliate_config', 'vm_canvas_executions', 'vm_canvas_assets'\n    ),\n    'executed_at', now()\n  );\n\n  RETURN v_result;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) FROM PUBLIC;\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) FROM authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) TO service_role;\n\nCOMMENT ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) IS\n  'Admin-only soft reset: wipes user-generated content/history/ledger while preserving profile, credentials, OAuth, Canvas Studio portfolio, and affiliate config. Sibling of delete_account (LGPD). Use case: clean E2E validation re-using existing account.';\n"
}

{

  "content": "-- Migration: 20260527005036_soft_reset_account_rpc_v2\n-- Description: Fix soft_reset_account RPC \u2014 remove reference to non-existent table content_mesh_asset.\n-- Background: v1 (20260527004739) incorrectly treated content_mesh_asset as a table; it is a node_type in mcorch_nodes,\n-- already covered by DELETE FROM mcorch_nodes. v1 attempt errored with PGRST205 / relation does not exist.\n-- This v2 redefines the function with corrected body and the same signature/permissions.\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(\n  p_user_id uuid,\n  p_new_balance int DEFAULT 10000\n)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nSET search_path = public\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  v_result jsonb;\n  v_content_count int;\n  v_runs_count int;\n  v_nodes_count int;\n  v_edges_count int;\n  v_conversations_count int;\n  v_scheduled_count int;\n  v_transactions_count int;\n  v_affiliate_links_count int;\n  v_profile_exists boolean;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE id = p_user_id) INTO v_profile_exists;\n  IF NOT v_profile_exists THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'soft_reset_account: profile % not found', p_user_id;\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_content_count          FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_runs_count             FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_nodes_count            FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_edges_count            FROM mcorch_edges       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_conversations_count    FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_scheduled_count        FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_transactions_count     FROM mcoin_transactions WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_affiliate_links_count  FROM affiliate_links    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n\n  -- Cascade delete in dependency order (child tables first).\n  -- NOTE: content_mesh_asset is a node_type in mcorch_nodes, NOT a separate table \u2014\n  -- wiped by DELETE FROM mcorch_nodes below.\n  DELETE FROM scheduled_posts    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM affiliate_links    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM aios_conversations WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcorch_edges       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcorch_nodes       WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM pipeline_runs      WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM content_library    WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n  DELETE FROM mcoin_transactions WHERE user_id = p_user_id;\n\n  UPDATE profiles\n     SET mco_balance = p_new_balance,\n         score       = 0,\n         updated_at  = now()\n   WHERE id = p_user_id;\n\n  -- PRESERVED (NOT deleted):\n  --   * profiles (row preserved, balance + score reset above)\n  --   * user_api_keys (per-user vault retained)\n  --   * social_accounts (OAuth tokens retained)\n  --   * affiliate_config (ML affiliate channel retained)\n  --   * vm_canvas_executions, vm_canvas_assets (Canvas Studio portfolio retained)\n  --   * mcorch_nodes/edges with user_id IS NULL (system audit trail preserved)\n  --   * infra_health_logs (global telemetry)\n\n  v_result := jsonb_build_object(\n    'reset', true,\n    'user_id', p_user_id,\n    'new_balance', p_new_balance,\n    'tables_affected', jsonb_build_object(\n      'content_library',    v_content_count,\n      'pipeline_runs',      v_runs_count,\n      'mcorch_nodes',       v_nodes_count,\n      'mcorch_edges',       v_edges_count,\n      'aios_conversations', v_conversations_count,\n      'scheduled_posts',    v_scheduled_count,\n      'mcoin_transactions', v_transactions_count,\n      'affiliate_links',    v_affiliate_links_count\n    ),\n    'preserved_tables', jsonb_build_array(\n      'profiles', 'user_api_keys', 'social_accounts',\n      'affiliate_config', 'vm_canvas_executions', 'vm_canvas_assets'\n    ),\n    'executed_at', now()\n  );\n\n  RETURN v_result;\nEND;\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) FROM PUBLIC;\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) FROM authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) TO service_role;\n\nCOMMENT ON FUNCTION public.soft_reset_account(uuid, int) IS\n  'Admin-only soft reset (v2): wipes user-generated content/history/ledger while preserving profile, credentials, OAuth, Canvas Studio portfolio, and affiliate config. Sibling of delete_account (LGPD). Use case: clean E2E validation re-using existing account.';\n"
}

> ```
> -- Migration: 20260526202000_async_orchestrate_step.sql
> -- Create a secure, restricted RPC to invoke the orchestrate-step function asynchronously via pg_net.
> 
> CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.async_orchestrate_step(
>   p_supabase_url text,
>   p_service_key text,
>   p_run_id uuid,
>   p_step text
> )
> RETURNS void
> LANGUAGE plpgsql
> SECURITY DEFINER
> AS $$
> BEGIN
>   PERFORM net.http_post(
>     url     := p_supabase_url || '/functions/v1/orchestrate-step',
>     headers := jsonb_build_object(
>       'Content-Type',  'application/json',
>       'Authorization', 'Bearer ' || p_service_key
>     ),
>     body    := jsonb_build_object(
>       'run_id', p_run_id::text,
>       'step', p_step
>     )
>   );
> END;
> $$;
> 
> -- Revoke execution from standard roles to prevent abuse/SSRF.
> -- Only the service_role/admin client can invoke this.
> REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.async_orchestrate_step(text, text, uuid, text) FROM public, authenticated, anon;
> ```

## 🤖 Claude *(01:04:12)*

Encontrei um **bloqueador real** no caminho do E2E. Vou documentar e oferecer 3 opções pra desbloquear:

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  ⚠️ FINDING MATERIAL — orchestrate-step JWT mismatch
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

**Sintoma:** após disparar `async_orchestrate_step(RUN_ID, "article_generation")` via REST com minha service-role key local, o pipeline_run permaneceu `status=running, steps=[]` por 90s. A direct invoke da edge function `orchestrate-step` retornou `{"error":"Unauthorized"}`.

**Diagnóstico material** (linhas 48-50 `orchestrate-step/index.ts`):
```ts
const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization");
const serviceKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")!;
if (authHeader !== `Bearer ${serviceKey}`) return 401;
```

A comparação é **exact match string**. Minha `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` do .env local funciona para PostgREST (testei: `mco_balance=10000` returned) mas é **rejeitada pela edge function**. Provável causa: Supabase platform pode estar injetando uma key diferente no edge runtime (rotation ou new vs legacy format), e essa nova key não está no meu .env.

Logo, **qualquer chamada externa** (não originada da própria `orchestrate-content` rodando dentro da plataforma) ao `async_orchestrate_step` morre no 1º hop do cascade.

**Estado pós-cleanup:**
- `mco_balance: 10000.00` ✅ (refund OK via `award_mco_coins`)
- `pipeline_runs` Sovereign: 0 (DELETE do órfão)
- `mcoin_transactions`: 2 rows (deduct + award — audit trail do experiment preservado)

**Observação anotada** (modo resiliente conforme sua diretiva).

```json
{
  "command": "set -a; source .env; set +a\nsleep 1; TS=$(date -u +\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\")\ncat > \"supabase/migrations/${TS}_async_orchestrate_step_debug.sql\" <<'EOF'\n-- Debug instrumentation: store metadata about async_orchestrate_step calls in a debug table.\n-- NEVER stores the full JWT \u2014 only prefix/suffix/length for diagnostic.\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.async_orchestrate_step_debug (\n  id bigserial PRIMARY KEY,\n  run_id uuid,\n  step text,\n  service_key_len int,\n  service_key_prefix text,\n  service_key_suffix text,\n  url text,\n  called_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\n);\nGRANT SELECT ON public.async_orchestrate_step_debug TO service_role;\n\n-- Re-create async_orchestrate_step with logging\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.async_orchestrate_step(\n  p_supabase_url text,\n  p_service_key text,\n  p_run_id uuid,\n  p_step text\n)\nRETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nSECURITY DEFINER\nAS $$\nBEGIN\n  INSERT INTO public.async_orchestrate_step_debug (\n    run_id, step, service_key_len, service_key_prefix, service_key_suffix, url\n  ) VALUES (\n    p_run_id,\n    p_step,\n    COALESCE(length(p_service_key), 0),\n    COALESCE(substring(p_service_key, 1, 16), 'NULL'),\n    COALESCE(substring(p_service_key FROM GREATEST(length(p_service_key)-9, 1)), 'NULL'),\n    p_supabase_url || '/functions/v1/orchestrate-step'\n  );\n\n  PERFORM net.http_post(\n    url     := p_supabase_url || '/functions/v1/orchestrate-step',\n    headers := jsonb_build_object(\n      'Content-Type',  'application/json',\n      'Authorization', 'Bearer ' || p_service_key\n    ),\n    body    := jsonb_build_object(\n      'run_id', p_run_id::text,\n      'step', p_step\n    )\n  );\nEND;\n$$;\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.async_orchestrate_step(text, text, uuid, text) FROM public, authenticated, anon;\nEOF\nnpx supabase db push --include-all 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Patch async_orchestrate_step with debug logging"
}
```

A edge function `orchestrate-content` está recebendo o **novo formato `sb_secret_*`** no `Deno.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (41 chars), mas o Gateway do Edge Runtime rejeita esse formato pra inter-function calls — espera o **legacy JWT `eyJ...`** (219 chars). ## 🤖 Claude *(01:28:50)*

**Pivot reconhecido e diagnóstico fechado**. Aqui está o cenário REAL:

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  ACHADO MATERIAL — Estado real dos agentes da Constelação
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

**Hoje os 137 agentes deployados (53 do Sovereign) NÃO estão trabalhando — são visual demo.**

| Componente | Estado real | Material proof |
|---|---|---|
| `crew_agents` schema | 16 columns (provider, model, system_prompt, priority, squad, status) | Rico — preparado para trabalho real |
| `useSimulation.ts` | **Fake** — flipa `status` aleatoriamente a cada 3s no Zustand client-side | `STATUS_POOL: ['active', 'thinking', 'waiting', 'idle', 'error']` random pick |
| Persistência de status | ❌ Nenhuma — Zustand client-side só | `updateAgentStatus()` não chama Supabase |
| `agent_executions` table | ❌ Não existe | Schema cache lista só `crew_agents`/`crew_edges`/`crew_module_ownership` |
| Token metering | ❌ Não existe | Nenhuma tabela de billing por agent |
| Runner edge function | ❌ Não existe | Nenhuma `agent-task-execute` ou similar |
| Custo atual real | **$0.00 / dia** | Zero chamadas reais a providers |
| "1 error" no print | Fake random | `useSimulation.ts:25` STATUS_POOL inclui 'error' |

**Tradução PT-BR**: a Constelação é hoje um teatro visual. Os agentes têm `system_prompt`, `provider`, `model` configurados (CEO=gpt-4, etc), mas nenhum loop conecta esses prompts a um runner real. Daí ficam todos IDLE com flips ocasionais — economia interna mcoCoins ainda **não tem nada para precificar**.

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  PROPOSTA — Constellation Economic Activation (CEA)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

**Princípio: menor custo possível como pré-requisito**, não como afterthought. Vou hardcoded **provider=groq, model=llama-3.3-70b-versatile** para o pilot (~$0.59/$0.79 por 1M tokens — quase free tier).

**Plano de 4 fases enxutas:**

| Fase | O que entrega | Custo até a próxima fase |
|---|---|---|
| **CEA-A · Schema metering** | Migration `agent_executions` (agent_id, run_id, prompt_chars, tokens_in, tokens_out, cost_usd_micro, cost_mcoin, model, provider, latency_ms, status, started_at, completed_at) + tabela `agent_pricing` com cents/1M tokens por (provider,model). | **$0** — só schema |
| **CEA-B · Runner edge** | `agent-task-execute` edge function: recebe `agent_id + task_prompt`, fetcha agent row, chama provider, captura usage, calcula cost, INSERT execution, UPDATE agent.status. JWT-guarded por Sovereign. | **$0** — só deploy |
| **CEA-C · Pilot run (Groq path)** | Forçar 4 Directors (1 por squad) a executar 1 task barata cada (~200 tokens output). Total esperado: **~3200 tokens · ~$0.002 USD · ~0.02 mcoCoins**. Captura: dashboard tokens/agent/day. | **~$0.002** |
| **CEA-D · Pricing dashboard** | `/dashboard/agent-economics`: tokens·day·agent, USD totais, mcoCoin equivalent (formula = `cost_usd * margin * 1000` → ex 200% margin = $0.005 = 5 mcoCoins/exec). Tier de squads. | **$0** |

**Pricing formula proposto** (mcoCoins):
```
exec_mcoin = ceil((tokens_in * price_in + tokens_out * price_out) * MARGIN_FACTOR * 1000)
```
- `MARGIN_FACTOR` = 2.0 (cobra 2x do custo real para sustentar infra + margem)
- Exemplo: Groq llama-70b · 500 tokens IN + 300 OUT = `(500*0.59 + 300*0.79) / 1M * 2 * 1000` ≈ **0.001 mcoCoin** (rounded up = 1 mcoCoin minimum charge)
- Exemplo CARO: gpt-4 · mesmo input = `(500*30 + 300*60) / 1M * 2 * 1000` ≈ **66 mcoCoins**

**Tarefas a criar:**
